birth.year\n    if (asof.month, asof.day) < (birth.month, birth.day):\n        age -= 1\n    return age\n\n\ndef normalize_phone_344(phone: str) -> str:\n    digits = re.sub(r\"\\D+\", \"\", phone or \"\")\n    # Expected mobile format: 11 digits (010xxxxxxxx). If it doesn't match, return best-effort original digits.\n    if len(digits) == 11:\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:11]}\"\n    if len(digits) == 10:\n        # Fall back: 3-3-4\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:6]}-{digits[6:10]}\"\n    return digits\n\n\ndef load_data() -> Dict[str, str]:\n    raw = json.loads(DATA_JSON.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError(\"patient_intake.json must be an object\")\n\n    # Convert all values to strings for substitution.\n    data: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in raw.items():\n        data[str(k)] = \"\" if v is None else str(v)\n\n    # Derived fields\n    birth_s = data.get(\"생년월일\", \"\")\n    visit_s = data.get(\"방문일\", \"\")\n    if birth_s and visit_s:\n        birth_d = parse_ymd(birth_s)\n        visit_d = parse_ymd(visit_s)\n        age = full_year_age_as_of(birth_d, visit_d)\n        data[\"생년월일\"] = f\"{birth_s} ({age}세)\"\n\n    phone_s = data.get(\"회신전화\", \"\")\n    if phone_s:\n        data[\"회신전화\"] = normalize_phone_344(phone_s)\n\n    return data\n\n\ndef iter_xml_files(root: Path) -> Iterable[Path]:\n    for p in root.rglob(\"*.xml\"):\n        if p.is_file():\n            yield p\n\n\ndef find_descendant_text_nodes(elem) -> List[etree._Element]:\n    # In HWPML, text nodes are typically <hp:t>. Second pass: if placeholders remain in paragraph text (possibly split across nodes),\n        # do a paragraph-level join/replace and consolidate into the first text node.\n        if t_nodes:\n            joined = \"\".join((t.text or \"\") for t in t_nodes)\n            if has_any_placeholder(joined):\n                new_joined, changed = replace_placeholders_in_text(joined, mapping)\n                if changed:\n                    t_nodes[0].text = new_joined\n                    for extra in t_nodes[1:]:\n                        # Keep element but clear text to avoid duplicating content.\n                        extra.text = \"\"\n                    paragraph_modified = True\n\n        if paragraph_modified:\n            remove_linesegarrays_from_paragraph(p)\n            file_modified = True\n\n    if file_modified:\n        xml_path.write_bytes(\n            etree.tostring(\n                tree,\n                encoding=\"UTF-8\",\n                xml_declaration=True,\n                pretty_print=False,\n            )\n        )\n\n    return file_modified\n\n\ndef verify_no_placeholders(root: Path) -> List[Tuple[Path, List[str]]]:\n    remaining: List[Tuple[Path, List[str]]] = []\n    for p in iter_xml_files(root):\n        txt = p.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\")\n        if \"{{\" in txt:\n            ph = sorted(set(re.findall(r\"\\{\\{[^}]+\\}\\}\", txt)))\n            remaining.append((p, ph))\n    return remaining\n\n\ndef repack_hwpx(template_hwpx: Path, extracted_dir: Path, output_hwpx: Path) -> None:\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(template_hwpx, \"r\") as zin:\n        names = zin.namelist()\n        # If some entries aren't extracted as files (e.g., directories), read from original.\n                data = zin.read(name)\n                info = zin.getinfo(name)\n                zi = zipfile.ZipInfo(filename=name, date_time=info.date_time)\n                zi.external_attr = info.external_attr\n                zi.compress_type = zipfile.ZIP_STORED if (name == \"mimetype\" and i == 0) else zipfile.ZIP_DEFLATED\n                zout.writestr(zi, data)\n                continue\n\n            # Expected mobile format: 11 digits (010xxxxxxxx). If it doesn't match, return best-effort original digits.\n    if len(digits) == 11:\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:11]}\"\n    if len(digits) == 10:\n        # Fall back: 3-3-4\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:6]}-{digits[6:10]}\"\n    return digits\n\n\ndef load_data() -> Dict[str, str]:\n    raw = json.loads(DATA_JSON.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError(\"patient_intake.json must be an object\")\n\n    # Convert all values to strings for substitution.\n    data: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in raw.items():\n        data[str(k)] = \"\" if v is None else str(v)\n\n    # Derived fields\n    birth_s = data.get(\"생년월일\", \"\")\n    visit_s = data.get(\"방문일\", \"\")\n    if birth_s and visit_s:\n        birth_d = parse_ymd(birth_s)\n        visit_d = parse_ymd(visit_s)\n        age = full_year_age_as_of(birth_d, visit_d)\n        data[\"생년월일\"] = f\"{birth_s} ({age}세)\"\n\n    phone_s = data.get(\"회신전화\", \"\")\n    if phone_s:\n        data[\"회신전화\"] = normalize_phone_344(phone_s)\n\n    return data\n\n\ndef iter_xml_files(root: Path) -> Iterable[Path]:\n    for p in root.rglob(\"*.xml\"):\n        if p.is_file():\n            yield p\n\n\ndef find_descendant_text_nodes(elem) -> List[etree._Element]:\n    # In HWPML, text nodes are typically <hp:t>. Korean full-year age (만 나이): year difference minus 1 if birthday hasn't occurred yet in that year.\n    age = asof.year - birth.year\n    if (asof.month, asof.day) < (birth.month, birth.day):\n        age -= 1\n    return age\n\n\ndef normalize_phone_344(phone: str) -> str:\n    digits = re.sub(r\"\\D+\", \"\", phone or \"\")\n    if len(digits) == 11:\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:7]}-{digits[7:11]}\"\n    if len(digits) == 10:\n        return f\"{digits[0:3]}-{digits[3:6]}-{digits[6:10]}\"\n    return digits\n\n\ndef load_data() -> Dict[str, str]:\n    raw = json.loads(DATA_JSON.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n    if not isinstance(raw, dict):\n        raise ValueError(\"patient_intake.json must be a JSON object\")\n\n    data: Dict[str, str] = {}\n    for k, v in raw.items():\n        data[str(k)] = \"\" if v is None else str(v)\n\n    # Preserve the original prefix if possible.\n        ET.register_namespace(\"hp\", uri)\n    return ns\n\n\ndef has_placeholder_text(s: str) -> bool:\n    return \"{{\" in s and \"}}\" in s\n\n\ndef replace_placeholders_in_text(s: str, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> Tuple[str, bool]:\n    changed = False\n    out = s\n    for k, v in mapping.items():\n        ph = \"{{\" + k + \"}}\"\n        if ph in out:\n            out = out.replace(ph, v)\n            changed = True\n    return out, changed\n\n\ndef find_text_elems(paragraph: ET.Element) -> List[ET.Element]:\n    # In HWPML, text nodes are typically <hp:t>.\n    return [e for e in paragraph.iter() if isinstance(e.tag, str) and localname(e.tag) == \"t\"]\n\n\ndef remove_linesegarray_from_paragraph(paragraph: ET.Element) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Remove linesegarray cache elements under this paragraph.\n\n    The task instruction says to remove <hp:linesegarray> elements (and their children)\n    from the modified paragraph.\n\n    ElementTree doesn't provide parent pointers, so we remove any direct child that is\n    a linesegarray. We also defensively remove nested linesegarray elements by scanning\n    direct children first (which matches typical HWPML structure).\n    \"\"\"\n    removed = 0\n    for child in list(paragraph):\n        if isinstance(child.tag, str) and localname(child.tag) == \"linesegarray\":\n            paragraph.remove(child)\n            removed += 1\n    return removed\n\n\ndef process_xml_file(xml_path: Path, mapping: Dict[str, str]) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Replace placeholders in one XML file.\n\n    Returns True if the file was modified.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Keep whitespace; avoid any pretty-print.\n    parser = ET.XMLParser()\n    tree = ET.parse(xml_path, parser=parser)\n    root = tree.getroot()\n\n    detect_namespaces(root)\n\n    file_modified = False\n\n    for elem in root.iter():\n        if not isinstance(elem.tag, str) or localname(elem.tag) != \"p\":\n            continue\n\n        paragraph_modified = False\n        t_elems = find_text_elems(elem)\n\n        # Some entries could be non-file entries; fall back to original bytes.\n                data = zin.read(name)\n\n            zout.writestr(zi, data)\n\n\ndef main() -> int:\n    if not TEMPLATE_HWPX.exists():\n        print(f\"ERROR: missing template: {TEMPLATE_HWPX}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n    if not DATA_JSON.exists():\n        print(f\"ERROR: missing data: {DATA_JSON}\", file=sys.stderr)\n        return 2\n\n    WORK_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n\n    try:\n        entry_order = extract_template()\n        mapping = load_data()\n\n        modified_files = 0\n        for xml_path in iter_xml_files(EXTRACTED_DIR):\n            if process_xml_file(xml_path, mapping):\n                modified_files += 1\n\n        remaining = verify_no_placeholders(EXTRACTED_DIR)\n        if remaining:\n            print(\"ERROR: Unreplaced placeholders remain:\", file=sys.stderr)\n            for p, ph in remaining:\n                print(f\"- {p}: {', '.join(ph)}\", file=sys.stderr)\n            return 3\n\n        repack_hwpx(entry_order, EXTRACTED_DIR, OUTPUT_HWPX)\n\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(\"ERROR:\", e, file=sys.stderr)\n        return 1\n\n    print(f\"OK: modified_xml_files={modified_files} output={OUTPUT_HWPX}\")\n    return 0\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    raise SystemExit(main())\n"}}],"model":"openai/gpt-5.2-20251211","stop_reason":null,"stop_details":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":0,"output_tokens":0,"cache_creation_input_tokens":null,"cache_read_input_tokens":null,"cache_creation":null,"inference_geo":null,"server_tool_use":null,"service_tier":null,"speed":"standard"},"provider":"OpenAI","context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"ecd40709-5611-4cea-8766-446027f6d2e2","uuid":"4d41c222-248a-4a47-953c-6f494bb09ec1"}
